Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The magnetometry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 78. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 934 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the magnetometry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 78 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 934. QED.
